Erworbener Wert und Finanzierung müssen gemeinsam erfasst werden; der volle Kaufpreis wird nicht isoliert abgezogen. Je 1.000 Mio. USD zusätzlicher NPV verändert sich der Wert um 1.21 USD/Aktie. Se deduce todo el coste SBC con un número fijo de acciones, sin una segunda penalización por dilución. Las 827 millones de acciones son un proxy del promedio ponderado diluido previsto para T3 AF27, no una cifra actual verificada. Los saldos son del 2026-07-31. Siguen pendientes efectivo, deuda, compras de Fin/Contentful, ASR final y evolución del número de acciones. Otros ingresos son un proxy incierto de ingresos de caja no operativos; los intereses de arrendamientos financieros incluidos en el ajuste total son una aproximación no cuantificada. No se deducen otra vez los pasivos por arrendamientos bajo esta convención de costes de caja. Las inversiones estratégicas se reconocen al 50% supuesto de su valor contable. Impuesto supuesto: 22.3%.

EV anunciado de Braze: 5,660 millones USD. El VPN incremental se supone en 0 millones USD; el análisis no demuestra ese valor. El valor adquirido y la financiación se deben contar juntos; no se deduce aisladamente todo el precio. Cada 1.000 millones USD de VPN incremental cambia el valor en 1.21 USD/acción. El crecimiento del FCFF no es crecimiento de ingresos o cRPO. Score, NGV y Regime Fit siguen siendo medidas separadas.

Insider coverage: CHECKED_WITH_EVENTS

Six Aug20 employee FCPE subscriptions and one Aug14 ordinary-share sale were disclosed. The issuer page listed only six documents; native register supplied the additional Olivier Charmeil filing.

Coverage means publicly available manager declarations in the native register at retrieval; it cannot establish undisclosed, exempt, below-threshold, or future late-reported activity. FCPE quantities are fund units (ISIN QS0004086268), not ordinary Sanofi shares. These subscriptions must not be combined with discretionary open-market stock purchases. Evidence and path diagnostics

REFLATION: 63+ consecutive sampled sessions. Growth change +0.8 pp; inflation change -18.5 pp from 2026-07-23 to 2026-10-20. 0 observed quadrant changes. All four proxies use common dates; this can differ from the legacy current reading. Historical market-proxy readings; descriptive, with no forecast probability.

Legacy Score includes valuation. Regime Fit includes a cover-based duration proxy. Business Quality uses growth, profitability, cash conversion, balance sheet and dilution; it is a separate heuristic and does not change existing actions or rankings.

Inspect data availability and valuation assumptions

UNVERIFIED means the cash-flow basis, dated source information, or a matching cash-flow/discount-rate contract is unestablished. MISMATCH means the declared cash-flow and discount-rate bases disagree. AVAILABLE describes presence, not accuracy or freshness. Missing subscores retain the legacy neutral balance/payout assumptions; Business Quality remains unavailable. Cushion compares only explicitly declared cash-flow growth. Revenue, earnings, bookings and production proxies remain descriptive and cannot impose a cushion penalty.

Buy limit = the lower of the reference quote and NGV / 0.60. This is the existing 60% cover rule, not a 60% discount. Prices use each listing's currency or quote unit (including GBp). Displayed prices are planning levels; confirm a fresh quote and the broker's permitted price increment.

The initial stop is a configurable percentage below the proposed entry (default 10%). Set it from the actual fill; never lower an existing stop just because this table refreshes. The default 90–180-day horizon is a planning assumption, not a predicted profitable exit date. Review sooner if the macro regime or investment thesis changes; a stop can trigger before that horizon.

These are new-entry scenarios, not instructions to replace stops on existing holdings. No orders are placed. Stops do not guarantee execution at the displayed price. How stop orders work.

Only dates strictly before the current exchange-local date are used, even after today’s close. This conservative policy can lag one completed session. At least 200 valid bars are required for metrics. Signal comparisons need at least 201 valid completed bars. BUY: previous close ≤ previous EMA 21, latest close > EMA 21 and SMA 200. SELL: previous close ≥ previous SMA 200, latest close < SMA 200. Events are labels for review; existing verdicts and actions are unchanged. EMA 21 uses an initial 21-close mean; ATR 14 uses a 14-true-range mean followed by Wilder smoothing (first TR = High − Low). More than 7 calendar days of lag is flagged STALE. The adjusted historical close can differ from the current quote. Values and an input-window hash are archived as observed; raw OHLC is not archived and provider adjustments can change on refetch. Performance advantage has not been established.

Eleven sectors, ten years

Annualised total return. The right-hand column is the same sector’s reflation affinity from the engine’s own table. Read them together: a decade where technology compounds and energy does not is a decade of falling discount rates, and a reflation tilt is a bet that the ordering inverts. Whether that is contrarian or late is the only question the table answers.
